+}
+
+/// Where a line of a MIR root's part is (spec 5.4 §4.3): MIR has no spans,
+/// so the report names the function and the block above the line, and the
+/// statement's index there, where other dumps give a source line. The part
+/// is walked line by line, as [`first_difference`] reads the lines both
+/// sides share, by the dump's indentation (ADR 0053 §1, ADR 0057 §2): at
+/// depth 0 the root's `(Mir` line and `(Diagnostics`; at depth 1 a
+/// `(Function` line, or an `(Extern` or a diagnostic's `(Error`; at depth 2
+/// a function's `(Temp` and `(Block` lines; at depth 3 a block's
+/// statements, then its terminator.
+#[derive(Clone, Default)]
+struct MirWalk {
+    /// Whether the part is a MIR root's: its first line starts `(Mir `.
+    mir: bool,
+    /// The function the last line walked is in, its name as the dump
+    /// quotes it; `None` outside any function.
+    function: Option<String>,
+    /// The block the last line walked is in, `bb<n>`; `None` outside any.
+    block: Option<String>,
+    /// The block's statement lines walked, its terminator counted as one.
+    statements: usize,
+}
+
+impl MirWalk {
+    /// Walk `line`, line `dump_line` of the root's part.
+    fn step(&mut self, dump_line: usize, line: &[u8]) {
+        if dump_line == 1 {
+            self.mir = line.starts_with(b"(Mir ");
+        }
+        if !self.mir {
+            return;
+        }
+        let line = text(line);
+        let line = line.trim_end_matches('\n');
+        let node = line.trim_start_matches(' ');
+        match (line.len() - node.len()) / 2 {
+            0 => {
+                self.function = None;
+                self.block = None;
+            }
+            1 => {
+                self.function = node
+                    .strip_prefix("(Function ")
+                    .and_then(|rest| rest.split(" params=").next())
+                    .map(str::to_string);
+                self.block = None;
+            }
+            2 => {
+                self.block = node
+                    .strip_prefix("(Block ")
+                    .map(|b| b.trim_end_matches(')').to_string());
+                self.statements = 0;
+            }
+            _ => self.statements += 1,
         }
     }
+
+    /// The place of `line`, the part's line `dump_line`, which differs,
+    /// after the lines walked, which both sides share, so either side's
+    /// text gives them. The line itself is S0's, or N's where S0's output
+    /// ended; its own kind decides what is named:
+    /// - the part's first line, the root's `(Mir` line: that alone;
+    /// - a line outside any function (an extern, MIR's diagnostics):
+    ///   "outside any function";
+    /// - a function's header: that function, by the differing line's name;
+    /// - a temp: the function, "at its temps";
+    /// - a block's header: the function and that block;
+    /// - a statement or a terminator: the function, the block, and its
+    ///   index in the block from 0, as `Block::stmts` counts, the
+    ///   terminator after the last statement.
+    ///
+    /// `None` for a part that is not a MIR root's.
+    fn place(&self, dump_line: usize, line: &[u8]) -> Option<String> {
+        if dump_line == 1 {
+            return line
+                .starts_with(b"(Mir ")
+                .then(|| "the root's first line".to_string());
+        }
+        if !self.mir {
+            return None;
+        }
+        let mut at = self.clone();
+        at.step(dump_line, line);
+        let depth = line.iter().take_while(|&&b| b == b' ').count() / 2;
+        Some(match (&at.function, &at.block, depth) {
+            (None, _, _) => "outside any function".to_string(),
+            (Some(f), None, 1) => format!("function {f}, at its header"),
+            (Some(f), None, _) => format!("function {f}, at its temps"),
+            (Some(f), Some(b), 2) => format!("function {f}, block {b}, at its header"),
+            (Some(f), Some(b), _) => {
+                format!("function {f}, block {b}, statement {}", self.statements)
+            }
+        })
+    }
 }
 
 /// `bytes` as text, each run that is not UTF-8 as a replacement character.
@@ -132,12 +241,18 @@ pub fn report(m: &Mismatch, dump: &Dump, dir: &Path, nova: &Path, selfhost: &Pat
     let at = if m.dump_line == 0 {
         "at its header: one side's output ended before it".to_string()
     } else {
-        let at_source =
-            m.s0.as_deref()
-                .or(m.n.as_deref())
-                .and_then(|line| source_line(dir, file, line))
-                .map(|l| format!(", source line {l}"))
-                .unwrap_or_default();
+        // A MIR dump's line has no span: its place stands for the source
+        // line.
+        let at_source = match &m.place {
+            Some(place) => format!(", {place}"),
+            None => {
+                m.s0.as_deref()
+                    .or(m.n.as_deref())
+                    .and_then(|line| source_line(dir, file, line))
+                    .map(|l| format!(", source line {l}"))
+                    .unwrap_or_default()
+            }
+        };
         format!("at line {} of its dump{at_source}", m.dump_line)
     };
     let (s0_args, n_args) = (dump.s0.join(" "), dump.n.join(" "));
diff --git a/selfhost/src/hir.nova b/selfhost/src/hir.nova
index ec1bf737..b7440c9f 100644
--- a/selfhost/src/hir.nova
+++ b/selfhost/src/hir.nova
@@ -763,3 +763,176 @@ pub type HirPattern =
     | PatVariant(Int, Int, Vec<HirPattern>)
     | PatTuple(Vec<HirPattern>)
     | PatOr(Vec<HirPattern>)
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// The module MIR reads (5.4)
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+/// Whether `ty` holds a generic parameter anywhere (`Ty::has_params`).
+pub fn has_params(ty: Ty) -> Bool {
+    match ty {
+        TyParam(_) => true
+        TyFn(params, ret) => {
+            for p in params.iter() { if has_params(p) { return true } }
+            has_params(ret)
+        }
+        TySum(_, args) => any_params(args)
+        TyRecord(_, args) => any_params(args)
+        TyArray(e) => has_params(e)
+        TyTuple(es) => any_params(es)
+        TyFuture(o) => has_params(o)
+        TyAssoc(on, _) => has_params(on)
+        _ => false
+    }
+}
+
+fn any_params(tys: Vec<Ty>) -> Bool {
+    for t in tys.iter() { if has_params(t) { return true } }
+    false
+}
+
+/// Whether a pattern is one the flat `match` lowering takes
+/// (`Pattern::is_flat`): a `_`, a binding, a literal, or a variant whose
+/// fields are each `_` or a binding.
+pub fn is_flat(pat: HirPattern) -> Bool {
+    match pat {
+        PatWildcard => true
+        PatBind(_) => true
+        PatInt(_) => true
+        PatBool(_) => true
+        PatStr(_) => true
+        PatChar(_) => true
+        PatVariant(_, _, fields) => {
+            for f in fields.iter() {
+                match f {
+                    PatWildcard => ()
+                    PatBind(_) => ()
+                    _ => return false
+                }
+            }
+            true
+        }
+        PatRange(_, _) => false
+        PatTuple(_) => false
+        PatOr(_) => false
+    }
+}
+
+/// A fully checked module: the type layouts and the typed functions
+/// (`Module`), as MIR reads it. `fn_index` is each function's position in
+/// `functions` by its id, which `function` reads where S0 scans: the same
+/// answer, a function's id being unique among them (spec 5.4 §6.2).
+pub record HirModule {
+    pub sums: Vec<SumType>,
+    pub records: Vec<RecordType>,
+    pub traits: Vec<TraitDef>,
+    pub impls: Vec<ImplInfo>,
+    pub functions: Vec<HirFunction>,
+    pub externs: Vec<ExternFn>,
+    pub fn_index: Map<Int, Int>,
+}
+
+/// The module of these tables and functions, its index built.
+pub fn hir_module(
+    sums: Vec<SumType>,
+    records: Vec<RecordType>,
+    traits: Vec<TraitDef>,
+    impls: Vec<ImplInfo>,
+    functions: Vec<HirFunction>,
+    externs: Vec<ExternFn>,
+) -> HirModule {
+    let mut index: Map<Int, Int> = Map::new()
+    for i in 0..functions.len() {
+        let id = functions.at(i).def_id
+        // The first, as S0's scan finds it.
+        if !index.contains_key(id) { let _ = index.insert(id, i) }
+    }
+    HirModule {
+        sums: sums,
+        records: records,
+        traits: traits,
+        impls: impls,
+        functions: functions,
+        externs: externs,
+        fn_index: index,
+    }
+}
+
+impl HirModule {
+    /// The extern function declared under `def_id` (`Module::extern_fn`).
+    pub fn extern_fn(self, def_id: Int) -> Option<ExternFn> {
+        for e in self.externs.iter() { if e.def_id == def_id { return Some(e) } }
+        None
+    }
+
+    /// The sum type declared under `def_id` (`Module::sum`).
+    pub fn sum(self, def_id: Int) -> Option<SumType> {
+        for s in self.sums.iter() { if s.def_id == def_id { return Some(s) } }
+        None
+    }
+
+    /// The record type declared under `def_id` (`Module::record`, whose
+    /// name is a keyword in Nova).
+    pub fn record_type(self, def_id: Int) -> Option<RecordType> {
+        for r in self.records.iter() { if r.def_id == def_id { return Some(r) } }
+        None
+    }
+
+    /// The trait declared under `def_id` (`Module::trait_def`).
+    pub fn trait_def(self, def_id: Int) -> Option<TraitDef> {
+        for t in self.traits.iter() { if t.def_id == def_id { return Some(t) } }
+        None
+    }
+
+    /// The function declared under `def_id` (`Module::function`).
+    pub fn function(self, def_id: Int) -> Option<HirFunction> {
+        match self.fn_index.get(def_id) {
+            Some(i) => Some(self.functions.at(i))
+            None => None
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Trait method `method` of `trait_id` for the concrete `self_ty`
+    /// resolved to the function it dispatches to, with the type arguments
+    /// that function is instantiated with (`Module::resolve_method_full`):
+    /// the fitting impl's method at the impl's own arguments, or the
+    /// trait's default body at `[self_ty]`; `None` when no impl fits.
+    pub fn resolve_method_full(
+        self,
+        trait_id: Int,
+        method: Int,
+        self_ty: Ty,
+    ) -> Option<(Int, Vec<Ty>)> {
+        let tr = match self.trait_def(trait_id) {
+            Some(t) => t
+            None => return None
+        }
+        if method < 0 || method >= tr.methods.len() { return None }
+        let method_name = tr.methods.at(method).name
+        let h = match head(self_ty) {
+            Some(h) => h
+            None => return None
+        }
+        let mut found: Option<(ImplInfo, Vec<Ty>)> = None
+        for i in self.impls.iter() {
+            if i.trait_id != trait_id || !head_eq(i.self_head, h) { continue }
+            match match_args(i, self_ty) {
+                Some(args) => {
+                    found = Some((i, args))
+                    break
+                }
+                None => ()
+            }
+        }
+        let (imp, impl_args) = match found {
+            Some(f) => f
+            None => return None
+        }
+        for (n, def) in imp.methods.iter() { if n == method_name { return Some((def, impl_args)) } }
+        let def = tr.methods.at(method).default_def
+        if def < 0 { return None }
+        let mut args: Vec<Ty> = Vec::new()
+        args.push(self_ty)
+        Some((def, args))
+    }
+}
diff --git a/selfhost/src/lower.nova b/selfhost/src/lower.nova
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..b8358c4e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/selfhost/src/lower.nova
@@ -0,0 +1,526 @@
+// HIR to MIR for one function, already monomorphized: a port of
+// crates/nova-mir/src/lower.rs, function by function and with the same
+// names (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-14-phase-5-4-mir-design.md
+// §6.1, ADR 0057). This file holds the lowerer's state, `lower_function`,
+// the helpers and `lower_expr`; lower_call.nova calls, trait calls,
+// operators, `if` and array repeat; lower_match.nova `match`.
+//
+// - `request`, S0's callback that queues a callee's instance, is the
+//   worklist itself, which the lowerer pushes onto as monomorphization's
+//   closure does.
+// - A span carries no file in N, so the lowerer keeps the file of the
+//   function it lowers, for the place of its one diagnostic.
+
+import diagnostic
+import hir
+import lower_call
+import lower_match
+import mir
+import token
+
+/// What lowering one function leaves while it runs (`Lowerer`).
+pub record Lowerer {
+    pub program: HirModule,
+    // The program entry point's id, named `main` rather than mangled.
+    pub entry: Int,
+    // The worklist, which each callee's instance joins (`request`).
+    pub worklist: Vec<(Int, Vec<Ty>)>,
+    pub temps: Vec<Int>,
+    pub blocks: Vec<BlockState>,
+    pub current: Int,
+    // Each local's temp, by the local's index.
+    pub local_map: Vec<Int>,
+    // `(continue_target, break_target)` of each enclosing loop.
+    pub loop_targets: Vec<(Int, Int)>,
+    pub diagnostics: Vec<Diagnostic>,
+    // The file of the function being lowered.
+    pub file: String,
+    // Each builtin's lowering, by its index (`builtin_lowerings`).
+    pub builtins: Vec<Lowering>,
+}
+
+/// A block being built (`BlockState`): its statements, and its terminator
+/// once it has one.
+pub record BlockState { pub stmts: Vec<MirStmt>, pub term: Option<MirTerm> }
+
+fn new_block_state() -> BlockState { BlockState { stmts: Vec::new(), term: None } }
+
+/// Lower a specialized (generic-free) function to MIR (`lower_function`):
+/// each direct call or function reference pushes its callee's instance
+/// onto `worklist`, as monomorphization asks; `builtins` is each builtin's
+/// lowering.
+pub fn lower_function(
+    func: HirFunction,
+    mangled: String,
+    program: HirModule,
+    entry: Int,
+    worklist: Vec<(Int, Vec<Ty>)>,
+    builtins: Vec<Lowering>,
+) -> Result<MirFunction, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
+    let mut blocks: Vec<BlockState> = Vec::new()
+    blocks.push(new_block_state())
+    let mut local_map: Vec<Int> = Vec::new()
+    for _ in 0..func.locals.len() { local_map.push(0) }
+    let mut lo = Lowerer {
+        program: program,
+        entry: entry,
+        worklist: worklist,
+        temps: Vec::new(),
+        blocks: blocks,
+        current: 0,
+        local_map: local_map,
+        loop_targets: Vec::new(),
+        diagnostics: Vec::new(),
+        file: func.span.file,
+        builtins: builtins,
+    }
+    let capture_count = func.capture_count
+    if func.takes_env {
+        // ABI: (env_ptr, real_params...). Temp 0 is the env pointer, then
+        // one temp per real parameter; captured locals and body locals get
+        // temps afterwards. Captured locals are loaded from the env at
+        // entry.
+        let env = lo.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+        for i in 0..func.params {
+            let local = func.locals.at(capture_count + i)
+            lo.local_map.set(capture_count + i, lo.new_temp(mir_ty(local.ty)))
+        }
+        let mut i = 0
+        while i < capture_count && i < func.locals.len() {
+            lo.local_map.set(i, lo.new_temp(mir_ty(func.locals.at(i).ty)))
+            i += 1
+        }
+        let mut k = capture_count + func.params
+        while k < func.locals.len() {
+            lo.local_map.set(k, lo.new_temp(mir_ty(func.locals.at(k).ty)))
+            k += 1
+        }
+        for c in 0..capture_count {
+            let ty = mir_ty(func.locals.at(c).ty)
+            if ty != MIR_UNIT {
+                let dst = lo.local_map.at(c)
+                lo.push(SRecordField(dst, env, c, ty))
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        // Normal ABI: parameters occupy the first temps in local order.
+        for i in 0..func.locals.len() {
+            let t = lo.new_temp(mir_ty(func.locals.at(i).ty))
+            if t != i { panic("params must map to leading temps") }
+            lo.local_map.set(i, t)
+        }
+    }
+    let body_ret = body_return_ty(func)
+    let result = lo.lower_expr(func.body)
+    if !diverges(func.body) {
+        if mir_ty(body_ret) == MIR_UNIT {
+            lo.terminate(TermReturn(-1))
+        } else {
+            lo.terminate(TermReturn(result))
+        }
+    }
+    if !lo.diagnostics.is_empty() { return Err(lo.diagnostics) }
+    let mut out: Vec<MirBlock> = Vec::new()
+    for i in 0..lo.blocks.len() {
+        let b = lo.blocks.at(i)
+        // Dead blocks (opened after divergence, never jumped to) may lack
+        // a terminator; they are unreachable, so trap.
+        let term = match b.term {
+            Some(t) => t
+            None => TermTrap
+        }
+        out.push(MirBlock { stmts: b.stmts, term: term })
+    }
+    Ok(
+        MirFunction {
+            name: mangled,
+            params: func.params,
+            takes_env: func.takes_env,
+            capture_count: func.capture_count,
+            temps: lo.temps,
+            ret: mir_ty(body_ret),
+            is_async: func.is_async,
+            blocks: out,
+        },
+    )
+}
+
+/// The type the function's body produces (`body_return_ty`): an `async
+/// fn`'s output, not the `Future` its signature returns.
+pub fn body_return_ty(func: HirFunction) -> Ty {
+    match func.ret_ty {
+        TyFuture(out) => if func.is_async { out } else { func.ret_ty }
+        other => other
+    }
+}
+
+/// Whether `e` never yields a value (`diverges`).
+pub fn diverges(e: HirExpr) -> Bool {
+    match e.ty {
+        TyNever => true
+        _ => false
+    }
+}
+
+impl Lowerer {
+    pub fn new_temp(mut self, ty: Int) -> Int {
+        let t = self.temps.len()
+        self.temps.push(ty)
+        t
+    }
+
+    pub fn new_block(mut self) -> Int {
+        let id = self.blocks.len()
+        self.blocks.push(new_block_state())
+        id
+    }
+
+    pub fn switch_to(mut self, id: Int) { self.current = id }
+
+    /// Add `stmt` to the current block, unless it is terminated already:
+    /// statements after a terminator are unreachable, and dropped.
+    pub fn push(mut self, stmt: MirStmt) {
+        let b = self.blocks.at(self.current)
+        if b.term.is_none() {
+            let mut stmts = b.stmts
+            stmts.push(stmt)
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Set the current block's terminator (first one wins).
+    pub fn terminate(mut self, term: MirTerm) {
+        let mut b = self.blocks.at(self.current)
+        if b.term.is_none() { b.term = Some(term) }
+    }
+
+    pub fn unit_temp(mut self) -> Int {
+        let t = self.new_temp(MIR_UNIT)
+        self.push(SConstUnit(t))
+        t
+    }
+
+    /// A runtime bounds check `check_bounds(index, len(arr))` that aborts
+    /// if `index` is out of range, before an array access.
+    pub fn bounds_check(mut self, arr: Int, index: Int) {
+        let len = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+        self.push(SArrayLen(len, arr))
+        self.push(SCallRuntime(-1, RT_CHECK_BOUNDS, Vec::from_array([index, len])))
+    }
+
+    /// Copy `src` into `dst` unless the value class is `Unit` (no data).
+    pub fn copy(mut self, dst: Int, src: Int) {
+        if self.temps.at(dst) != MIR_UNIT { self.push(SCopy(dst, src)) }
+    }
+
+    /// The symbol of a call to `def` at `type_args`, its instance queued
+    /// (`callee_name`): `main` for the entry point, an extern's raw
+    /// symbol, or the mangled name.
+    pub fn callee_name(mut self, def: Int, type_args: Vec<Ty>) -> String {
+        self.worklist.push((def, type_args))
+        if def == self.entry { return "main" }
+        match self.program.extern_fn(def) {
+            Some(ext) => return ext.symbol
+            None => ()
+        }
+        let name = match self.program.function(def) {
+            Some(f) => f.name
+            None => "__unknown_${def}"
+        }
+        mangle(def, name, type_args)
+    }
+
+    /// Lower `e`, returning the temp that holds its value (`lower_expr`).
+    pub fn lower_expr(mut self, e: HirExpr) -> Int {
+        match e.kind {
+            EkIntLit(v) => {
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+                self.push(SConstInt(t, v))
+                t
+            }
+            EkFloatLit(v) => {
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_F64)
+                self.push(SConstFloat(t, v))
+                t
+            }
+            EkBoolLit(v) => {
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_I8)
+                self.push(SConstBool(t, v))
+                t
+            }
+            EkStrLit(s) => {
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+                self.push(SConstStr(t, s))
+                t
+            }
+            EkCharLit(c) => {
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+                self.push(SConstInt(t, c.to_int()))
+                t
+            }
+            EkUnit => self.unit_temp()
+            EkLocal(l) => self.local_map.at(l)
+            EkMakeClosure(func, type_args, captures) => {
+                let code = self.callee_name(func, type_args)
+                let cap_temps = self.typed_exprs(captures)
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+                self.push(SMakeClosure(t, code, cap_temps))
+                t
+            }
+            EkCall(func, type_args, args) => self.lower_call(e, func, type_args, args)
+            EkMakeVariant(_, variant, args) => {
+                let fields = self.typed_exprs(args)
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+                self.push(SMakeSum(t, variant, fields))
+                t
+            }
+            // A tuple is an anonymous record: the same `MakeRecord`, so no
+            // backend sees a tuple.
+            EkMakeRecord(_, fields) => self.make_record(fields)
+            EkMakeTuple(elems) => self.make_record(elems)
+            EkFieldGet(target, index) => {
+                let rec = self.lower_expr(target)
+                let ty = mir_ty(e.ty)
+                if ty == MIR_UNIT { return self.unit_temp() }
+                let t = self.new_temp(ty)
+                self.push(SRecordField(t, rec, index, ty))
+                t
+            }
+            EkFieldSet(target, index, value) => {
+                // Left-to-right, as `IndexSet` does: the record expression
+                // is evaluated before the value expression.
+                let rec = self.lower_expr(target)
+                let v = self.lower_expr(value)
+                let ty = mir_ty(value.ty)
+                // A unit-typed field has no runtime representation, so
+                // there is nothing to store.
+                if ty != MIR_UNIT { self.push(SSetField(rec, index, v, ty)) }
+                self.unit_temp()
+            }
+            EkMakeArray(elems) => {
+                let lowered = self.typed_exprs(elems)
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+                self.push(SMakeArray(t, lowered))
+                t
+            }
+            EkArrayRepeat(init, len) => self.lower_array_repeat(init, len)
+            EkArrayLen(target) => {
+                let arr = self.lower_expr(target)
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+                self.push(SArrayLen(t, arr))
+                t
+            }
+            EkIndex(target, index) => {
+                let arr = self.lower_expr(target)
+                let idx = self.lower_expr(index)
+                self.bounds_check(arr, idx)
+                let ty = mir_ty(e.ty)
+                if ty == MIR_UNIT { return self.unit_temp() }
+                let t = self.new_temp(ty)
+                self.push(SArrayGet(t, arr, idx, ty))
+                t
+            }
+            EkIndexSet(target, index, value) => {
+                let arr = self.lower_expr(target)
+                let idx = self.lower_expr(index)
+                let v = self.lower_expr(value)
+                self.bounds_check(arr, idx)
+                let ty = mir_ty(value.ty)
+                if ty != MIR_UNIT { self.push(SArraySet(arr, idx, v, ty)) }
+                self.unit_temp()
+            }
+            EkTraitCall(
+                trait_id,
+                method,
+                self_ty,
+                type_args,
+                receiver,
+                args,
+            ) => self.lower_trait_call(e, trait_id, method, self_ty, type_args, receiver, args)
+            EkBinary(op, lhs, rhs) => self.lower_binary(op, lhs, rhs)
+            EkLogicalAnd(lhs, rhs) => self.lower_logical(lhs, rhs, true)
+            EkLogicalOr(lhs, rhs) => self.lower_logical(lhs, rhs, false)
+            EkUnary(op, inner) => {
+                let src = self.lower_expr(inner)
+                match op {
+                    OpNeg => {
+                        let class = operand_class(inner.ty)
+                        let t = self.new_temp(self.temps.at(src))
+                        self.push(SNeg(t, class, src))
+                        t
+                    }
+                    OpNot => {
+                        let t = self.new_temp(MIR_I8)
+                        self.push(SNot(t, src))
+                        t
+                    }
+                    OpBitNot => {
+                        let t = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+                        self.push(SBitNot(t, src))
+                        t
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            EkLet(local, init) => {
+                let v = self.lower_expr(init)
+                if !diverges(init) { self.copy(self.local_map.at(local), v) }
+                self.unit_temp()
+            }
+            EkAssign(local, value) => {
+                let v = self.lower_expr(value)
+                if !diverges(value) { self.copy(self.local_map.at(local), v) }
+                self.unit_temp()
+            }
+            EkBlock(stmts, trailing) => {
+                for i in 0..stmts.len() { let _ = self.lower_expr(stmts.at(i)) }
+                match trailing {
+                    Some(t) => self.lower_expr(t)
+                    None => self.unit_temp()
+                }
+            }
+            EkIf(cond, then, else_) => self.lower_if(e, cond, then, else_)
+            EkWhile(cond, body) => {
+                let header = self.new_block()
+                let body_b = self.new_block()
+                let exit = self.new_block()
+                self.terminate(TermGoto(header))
+                self.switch_to(header)
+                // `continue` to the header (re-test), `break` to the exit;
+                // pushed before the condition is lowered, so a break or
+                // continue in the condition targets this loop.
+                self.loop_targets.push((header, exit))
+                let c = self.lower_expr(cond)
+                if diverges(cond) {
+                    // The condition never yields a bool; control has left
+                    // the header already. Resume at the exit.
+                    let _ = self.loop_targets.pop()
+                    self.switch_to(exit)
+                    return self.unit_temp()
+                }
+                self.terminate(TermBranch(c, body_b, exit))
+                self.switch_to(body_b)
+                let _ = self.lower_expr(body)
+                let _ = self.loop_targets.pop()
+                self.terminate(TermGoto(header))
+                self.switch_to(exit)
+                self.unit_temp()
+            }
+            EkBreak => self.lower_jump(true)
+            EkContinue => self.lower_jump(false)
+            EkMatch(scrutinee, arms) => self.lower_match(e, scrutinee, arms)
+            EkReturn(value) => {
+                match value {
+                    Some(v) => {
+                        let t = self.lower_expr(v)
+                        if !diverges(v) {
+                            if self.temps.at(t) == MIR_UNIT {
+                                self.terminate(TermReturn(-1))
+                            } else {
+                                self.terminate(TermReturn(t))
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                    None => self.terminate(TermReturn(-1))
+                }
+                // Continue lowering into a fresh (unreachable) block.
+                let dead = self.new_block()
+                self.switch_to(dead)
+                self.unit_temp()
+            }
+            EkToStr(inner) => {
+                let v = self.lower_expr(inner)
+                let func = match inner.ty {
+                    TyInt => RT_INT_TO_STR
+                    TyFloat => RT_FLOAT_TO_STR
+                    TyBool => RT_BOOL_TO_STR
+                    TyChar => RT_CHAR_TO_STR
+                    // String / error recovery: pass through.
+                    _ => return v
+                }
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+                self.push(SCallRuntime(t, func, Vec::from_array([v])))
+                t
+            }
+            EkStrConcat(parts) => self.lower_str_concat(parts)
+            EkAwait(inner) => {
+                // A marker statement, not control flow: performing the
+                // await is `async_lower`'s block split.
+                let future = self.lower_expr(inner)
+                let ty = mir_ty(e.ty)
+                let dst = if ty == MIR_UNIT { -1 } else { self.new_temp(ty) }
+                self.push(SAwait(dst, future))
+                // A unit-output await produces no value, as a
+                // unit-returning call: the result is a fresh unit.
+                if dst >= 0 { dst } else { self.unit_temp() }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Each of `exprs` lowered, in order, beside its class.
+    fn typed_exprs(mut self, exprs: Vec<HirExpr>) -> Vec<(Int, Int)> {
+        let mut out: Vec<(Int, Int)> = Vec::new()
+        for i in 0..exprs.len() {
+            let x = exprs.at(i)
+            let t = self.lower_expr(x)
+            out.push((t, mir_ty(x.ty)))
+        }
+        out
+    }
+
+    // A record's or a tuple's `MakeRecord`.
+    fn make_record(mut self, fields: Vec<HirExpr>) -> Int {
+        let lowered = self.typed_exprs(fields)
+        let t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+        self.push(SMakeRecord(t, lowered))
+        t
+    }
+
+    // `break` (`is_break`) or `continue`: a jump to the enclosing loop's
+    // target, then lowering goes on in a fresh dead block.
+    fn lower_jump(mut self, is_break: Bool) -> Int {
+        if !self.loop_targets.is_empty() {
+            let (cont, brk) = self.loop_targets.last()
+            self.terminate(TermGoto(if is_break { brk } else { cont }))
+        }
+        let dead = self.new_block()
+        self.switch_to(dead)
+        self.unit_temp()
+    }
+
+    // An interpolation's parts concatenated: none, an empty string; three
+    // or more, one array and one n-ary concatenation; otherwise pairwise.
+    fn lower_str_concat(mut self, parts: Vec<HirExpr>) -> Int {
+        if parts.is_empty() {
+            let t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+            self.push(SConstStr(t, ""))
+            return t
+        }
+        if parts.len() >= 3 {
+            let mut elems: Vec<(Int, Int)> = Vec::new()
+            for i in 0..parts.len() { elems.push((self.lower_expr(parts.at(i)), MIR_PTR)) }
+            let arr = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+            self.push(SMakeArray(arr, elems))
+            let t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+            self.push(SCallRuntime(t, RT_STR_CONCAT_N, Vec::from_array([arr])))
+            return t
+        }
+        let mut acc = self.lower_expr(parts.at(0))
+        let mut i = 1
+        while i < parts.len() {
+            let rhs = self.lower_expr(parts.at(i))
+            let t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+            self.push(SCallRuntime(t, RT_STR_CONCAT, Vec::from_array([acc, rhs])))
+            acc = t
+            i += 1
+        }
+        acc
+    }
+}
+
+/// A binary or unary operation's operand class (`operand_class`).
+pub fn operand_class(ty: Ty) -> Int {
+    match ty {
+        TyFloat => OC_FLOAT
+        TyBool => OC_BOOL
+        _ => OC_INT
+    }
+}
diff --git a/selfhost/src/lower_call.nova b/selfhost/src/lower_call.nova
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..1da2e66e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/selfhost/src/lower_call.nova
@@ -0,0 +1,476 @@
+// Calls, trait calls, operators, `if` and array repeat: lower.rs's
+// `lower_call` with its builtin table, `lower_trait_call`,
+// `lower_binary`, `lower_logical`, `lower_if`, `lower_array_repeat`,
+// `panic_if` and `bin_i64`, function by function (spec
+// docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-14-phase-5-4-mir-design.md §6.1, ADR
+// 0057).
+//
+// - S0's exhaustive `match b` over the builtins is `BUILTIN_LOWERING`, one
+//   entry per builtin in `BUILTIN_VARIANTS`' order: the runtime function a
+//   builtin calls, or `=Reinterpret`, `=FutureOutput` or `=Mem` and its
+//   memory operation. Monomorphization reads it once into a list of
+//   `Lowering`s (`builtin_lowerings`), which every function's lowering
+//   shares.
+// - The `Mem` builtins are the WASM target's alone (`mem_load8` in a
+//   native program is E0001, before MIR): N meets none, and panics if it
+//   does.
+
+import builtins
+import diagnostic
+import hir
+import lower
+import mir
+import token
+
+/// How one builtin reaches its implementation (`Lowering`).
+pub type Lowering =
+    // one `CallRuntime` to this runtime function
+    | LoweringRuntime(Int)
+    // the result is the argument, in another type of the same class
+    | LoweringReinterpret
+    // two typed field loads reaching a finished future's output slot
+    | LoweringFutureOutput
+    // a WASM memory access in line, by its operation's name
+    | LoweringMem(String)
+
+/// Each builtin's lowering, `BUILTIN_VARIANTS`' order: the arms of
+/// `lower_call`'s `match b`.
+pub const BUILTIN_LOWERING: [String] = [
+    "Println",
+    "Print",
+    "EPrint",
+    "EPrintln",
+    "Panic",
+    "StrCmp",
+    "StrHash",
+    "=Reinterpret",
+    "StrLenChars",
+    "StrChars",
+    "StrFromChars",
+    "StrJoin",
+    "StrIndexOf",
+    "JsonQuote",
+    "StrToUpper",
+    "StrToLower",
+    "FloatFixed",
+    "StrToFloat",
+    "StrIsFloat",
+    "FloatToBits",
+    "=Reinterpret",
+    "=Reinterpret",
+    "TestSelector",
+    "TaskSpawn",
+    "TaskIsDone",
+    "TaskRelease",
+    "TaskBlockOn",
+    "=FutureOutput",
+    "TaskYieldFuture",
+    "TaskSleepFutureNanos",
+    "TaskJoinFuture",
+    "TaskTimeoutFuture",
+    "FsReadToString",
+    "FsWriteString",
+    "FsTakeString",
+    "FsLastErrorMessage",
+    "FsTempDir",
+    "FsExists",
+    "FsCreateDir",
+    "FsCreateDirAll",
+    "FsRemoveFile",
+    "FsRemoveDirAll",
+    "FsRename",
+    "FsReadDir",
+    "FsTakeStringArray",
+    "FsKind",
+    "FsRead",
+    "FsTakeBytes",
+    "FsWrite",
+    "FileOpen",
+    "FileClose",
+    "FileRead",
+    "FileWrite",
+    "FileFlush",
+    "IoStdinRead",
+    "IoStdoutWrite",
+    "IoStderrWrite",
+    "IoStdoutFlush",
+    "IoStderrFlush",
+    "NetConnect",
+    "NetClose",
+    "NetRead",
+    "NetWrite",
+    "NetReadTimeout",
+    "NetListen",
+    "NetLocalPort",
+    "NetAccept",
+    "BytesLen",
+    "BytesFromString",
+    "BytesIsUtf8",
+    "BytesToStringUnchecked",
+    "BytesAt",
+    "BytesSlice",
+    "BytesConcat",
+    "BytesToInts",
+    "BytesFromInts",
+    "BytesEq",
+    "IntHashSeed",
+    "HttpParseRequest",
+    "CryptoHash",
+    "CryptoRandomBytes",
+    "CryptoRandomInt",
+    "CryptoPbkdf2Sha256",
+    "CryptoPbkdf2Sha256Verify",
+    "TimeNowNanos",
+    "TimeNowEpochNanos",
+    "LogConfigLevel",
+    "LogConfigToStderr",
+    "LogSetConfig",
+    "ShutdownRequested",
+    "ProcessArgCount",
+    "ProcessArg",
+    "ProcessExit",
+    "ProcessRun",
+    "ProcessTakeStatus",
+    "ProcessTakeStdout",
+    "ProcessTakeStderr",
+    "ProcessEnv",
+    "ProcessCurrentExe",
+    "UiStatePresent",
+    "UiStateLoad",
+    "UiStateStore",
+    "RouterStatePresent",
+    "RouterStateLoad",
+    "RouterStateStore",
+    "=Mem Load8",
+    "=Mem Store8",
+    "=Mem Load64",
+    "=Mem Store64",
+    "=Mem Copy",
+    "=Mem Fill",
+    "=Mem Pages",
+    "=Mem Grow",
+    "=Mem HeapBase",
+    "=Mem Poll",
+    "=Mem Abort",
+    "=Mem ClosureWord",
+    "=Mem ClosureCall",
+]
+
+/// `BUILTIN_LOWERING` read into `Lowering`s, by the builtin's index.
+pub fn builtin_lowerings() -> Vec<Lowering> {
+    let mut out: Vec<Lowering> = Vec::new()
+    for entry in BUILTIN_LOWERING.iter() {
+        let how = if entry == "=Reinterpret" {
+            LoweringReinterpret
+        } else if entry == "=FutureOutput" {
+            LoweringFutureOutput
+        } else if entry.starts_with("=Mem ") {
+            LoweringMem(entry.byte_slice(5, entry.byte_len()))
+        } else {
+            LoweringRuntime(rt_func(entry))
+        }
+        out.push(how)
+    }
+    out
+}
+
+impl Lowerer {
+    /// A call: its arguments, then the call to a function, a builtin or a
+    /// function value (`lower_call`).
+    pub fn lower_call(
+        mut self,
+        e: HirExpr,
+        func: Callee,
+        type_args: Vec<Ty>,
+        args: Vec<HirExpr>,
+    ) -> Int {
+        let mut arg_temps: Vec<Int> = Vec::new()
+        for i in 0..args.len() { arg_temps.push(self.lower_expr(args.at(i))) }
+        let ret_class = mir_ty(e.ty)
+        let dst = if ret_class == MIR_UNIT { -1 } else { self.new_temp(ret_class) }
+        match func {
+            CalleeDef(def) => {
+                let callee = self.callee_name(def, type_args)
+                self.push(SCall(dst, callee, arg_temps))
+            }
+            CalleeBuiltin(b) => {
+                match self.builtins.at(b) {
+                    LoweringRuntime(f) => self.push(SCallRuntime(dst, f, arg_temps))
+                    LoweringReinterpret => {
+                        // A representation-level no-op, a register move: the
+                        // `Copy` is not optional, or every later reader of the
+                        // result reads an unassigned temp.
+                        if dst < 0 || arg_temps.len() != 1 {
+                            panic(
+                                "`${BUILTIN_NAMES[b]}` must lower with a dst and exactly one argument (got dst=${dest_debug(dst)}, ${arg_temps.len()} args), or its result temp is left unassigned",
+                            )
+                        }
+                        self.push(SCopy(dst, arg_temps.at(0)))
+                    }
+                    LoweringFutureOutput => {
+                        // `task_output(fut)`: the future's state object, then
+                        // that object's output slot, loaded in the output's
+                        // own class; nothing for a unit output.
+                        if arg_temps.len() != 1 {
+                            panic("`${BUILTIN_NAMES[b]}` takes exactly one future")
+                        }
+                        if dst >= 0 {
+                            let state = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+                            self.push(
+                                SRecordField(state, arg_temps.at(0), FUTURE_SLOT_STATE, MIR_PTR),
+                            )
+                            self.push(SRecordField(dst, state, STATE_SLOT_OUTPUT, ret_class))
+                        }
+                    }
+                    LoweringMem(op) => panic(
+                        "`${BUILTIN_NAMES[b]}` lowers to the WASM memory access `${op}`, which N's native MIR does not have",
+                    )
+                }
+            }
+            CalleeLocal(l) => {
+                let callee = self.local_map.at(l)
+                let mut params: Vec<Int> = Vec::new()
+                for i in 0..args.len() { params.push(mir_ty(args.at(i).ty)) }
+                self.push(SCallIndirect(dst, callee, params, ret_class, arg_temps))
+            }
+        }
+        if dst >= 0 { dst } else { self.unit_temp() }
+    }
+
+    /// A trait method call, statically dispatched: `self_ty` is concrete
+    /// here, so the call goes to the impl's method or the trait's default
+    /// body (`lower_trait_call`). A trait associated function has no
+    /// receiver.
+    pub fn lower_trait_call(
+        mut self,
+        e: HirExpr,
+        trait_id: Int,
+        method: Int,
+        self_ty: Ty,
+        method_type_args: Vec<Ty>,
+        receiver: Option<HirExpr>,
+        args: Vec<HirExpr>,
+    ) -> Int {
+        let mut arg_temps: Vec<Int> = Vec::new()
+        match receiver {
+            Some(r) => arg_temps.push(self.lower_expr(r))
+            None => ()
+        }
+        for i in 0..args.len() { arg_temps.push(self.lower_expr(args.at(i))) }
+        let ret_class = mir_ty(e.ty)
+        let dst = if ret_class == MIR_UNIT { -1 } else { self.new_temp(ret_class) }
+        let (target, type_args) = match self.program.resolve_method_full(
+            trait_id,
+            method,
+            self_ty,
+        ) {
+            Some(r) => r
+            None => {
+                let trait_name = match self.program.trait_def(trait_id) {
+                    Some(t) => t.name
+                    None => "trait#${trait_id}"
+                }
+                self.diagnostics.push(
+                    error_at(
+                        "E0013",
+                        "no implementation of trait `${trait_name}` for the receiver type",
+                        self.file,
+                        e.span,
+                        "no matching trait impl",
+                    ),
+                )
+                return if dst >= 0 { dst } else { self.unit_temp() }
+            }
+        }
+        // The trait method's own generic arguments after the impl's or
+        // `Self`'s.
+        let mut all = type_args
+        for t in method_type_args.iter() { all.push(t) }
+        let callee = self.callee_name(target, all)
+        self.push(SCall(dst, callee, arg_temps))
+        if dst >= 0 { dst } else { self.unit_temp() }
+    }
+
+    /// A binary operation (`lower_binary`): string equality and `+` are
+    /// runtime calls, a unit comparison a constant, every other one `Bin`.
+    pub fn lower_binary(mut self, op: HirBinOp, lhs: HirExpr, rhs: HirExpr) -> Int {
+        let l = self.lower_expr(lhs)
+        let r = self.lower_expr(rhs)
+        let is_string = match lhs.ty {
+            TyString => true
+            _ => false
+        }
+        let (is_eq, is_ne, is_add) = match op {
+            OpEq => (true, false, false)
+            OpNe => (false, true, false)
+            OpAdd => (false, false, true)
+            _ => (false, false, false)
+        }
+        if is_string && (is_eq || is_ne) {
+            let eq = self.new_temp(MIR_I8)
+            self.push(SCallRuntime(eq, RT_STR_EQ, Vec::from_array([l, r])))
+            if is_eq { return eq }
+            let ne = self.new_temp(MIR_I8)
+            self.push(SNot(ne, eq))
+            return ne
+        }
+        // `String + String` is one call of the runtime's concatenation.
+        if is_string && is_add {
+            let t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+            self.push(SCallRuntime(t, RT_STR_CONCAT, Vec::from_array([l, r])))
+            return t
+        }
+        // Unit comparison is a constant.
+        match lhs.ty {
+            TyUnit => {
+                let t = self.new_temp(MIR_I8)
+                self.push(SConstBool(t, is_eq))
+                return t
+            }
+            _ => ()
+        }
+        let class = operand_class(lhs.ty)
+        let result = if is_comparison(op) {
+            MIR_I8
+        } else if class == OC_FLOAT {
+            MIR_F64
+        } else {
+            MIR_I64
+        }
+        let t = self.new_temp(result)
+        self.push(SBin(t, op, class, l, r))
+        t
+    }
+
+    /// `&&` (`is_and`) or `||`, as control flow (`lower_logical`).
+    pub fn lower_logical(mut self, lhs: HirExpr, rhs: HirExpr, is_and: Bool) -> Int {
+        let result = self.new_temp(MIR_I8)
+        let l = self.lower_expr(lhs)
+        if diverges(lhs) { return result }
+        let rhs_block = self.new_block()
+        let short_block = self.new_block()
+        let join = self.new_block()
+        if is_and {
+            self.terminate(TermBranch(l, rhs_block, short_block))
+        } else {
+            self.terminate(TermBranch(l, short_block, rhs_block))
+        }
+        self.switch_to(rhs_block)
+        let r = self.lower_expr(rhs)
+        if !diverges(rhs) { self.copy(result, r) }
+        self.terminate(TermGoto(join))
+        self.switch_to(short_block)
+        self.push(SConstBool(result, !is_and))
+        self.terminate(TermGoto(join))
+        self.switch_to(join)
+        result
+    }
+
+    /// `if`, with or without an `else` (`lower_if`).
+    pub fn lower_if(
+        mut self,
+        e: HirExpr,
+        cond: HirExpr,
+        then: HirExpr,
+        else_: Option<HirExpr>,
+    ) -> Int {
+        let result = self.new_temp(mir_ty(e.ty))
+        let c = self.lower_expr(cond)
+        if diverges(cond) { return result }
+        let then_b = self.new_block()
+        let join = self.new_block()
+        let else_b = if else_.is_some() { self.new_block() } else { join }
+        self.terminate(TermBranch(c, then_b, else_b))
+        self.switch_to(then_b)
+        let tv = self.lower_expr(then)
+        if !diverges(then) { self.copy(result, tv) }
+        self.terminate(TermGoto(join))
+        match else_ {
+            Some(else_expr) => {
+                self.switch_to(else_b)
+                let ev = self.lower_expr(else_expr)
+                if !diverges(else_expr) { self.copy(result, ev) }
+                self.terminate(TermGoto(join))
+            }
+            None => ()
+        }
+        self.switch_to(join)
+        result
+    }
+
+    /// `[init; n]`: allocate `n` slots behind two length guards, then fill
+    /// every one with `init`, lowered once (`lower_array_repeat`).
+    pub fn lower_array_repeat(mut self, init: HirExpr, len: HirExpr) -> Int {
+        let init_t = self.lower_expr(init)
+        let len_t = self.lower_expr(len)
+        let elem_ty = mir_ty(init.ty)
+        let arr = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+        let zero = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+        self.push(SConstInt(zero, 0))
+        let negative = self.bin_i64(OpLt, len_t, zero, MIR_I8)
+        self.panic_if(negative, "array length must not be negative")
+        let max = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+        self.push(SConstInt(max, MAX_ARRAY_LEN))
+        let too_long = self.bin_i64(OpGt, len_t, max, MIR_I8)
+        self.panic_if(too_long, "array length is too large")
+        self.push(SArrayAlloc(arr, len_t))
+        // A unit element has no runtime representation: nothing to store.
+        if elem_ty == MIR_UNIT { return arr }
+        let i = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+        self.push(SConstInt(i, 0))
+        let header = self.new_block()
+        let body_b = self.new_block()
+        let exit = self.new_block()
+        self.terminate(TermGoto(header))
+        self.switch_to(header)
+        let more = self.bin_i64(OpLt, i, len_t, MIR_I8)
+        self.terminate(TermBranch(more, body_b, exit))
+        self.switch_to(body_b)
+        self.push(SArraySet(arr, i, init_t, elem_ty))
+        let one = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+        self.push(SConstInt(one, 1))
+        let next = self.bin_i64(OpAdd, i, one, MIR_I64)
+        self.push(SCopy(i, next))
+        self.terminate(TermGoto(header))
+        self.switch_to(exit)
+        arr
+    }
+
+    /// `if bad { panic(msg) }`, continuing in a fresh block (`panic_if`).
+    pub fn panic_if(mut self, bad: Int, msg: String) {
+        let panic_b = self.new_block()
+        let ok_b = self.new_block()
+        self.terminate(TermBranch(bad, panic_b, ok_b))
+        self.switch_to(panic_b)
+        let m = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+        self.push(SConstStr(m, msg))
+        self.push(SCallRuntime(-1, RT_PANIC, Vec::from_array([m])))
+        // The runtime's panic aborts, so control never returns here.
+        self.terminate(TermTrap)
+        self.switch_to(ok_b)
+    }
+
+    /// An `Int`-class binary operation on two temps, into a fresh temp of
+    /// class `result` (`bin_i64`).
+    pub fn bin_i64(mut self, op: HirBinOp, lhs: Int, rhs: Int, result: Int) -> Int {
+        let dst = self.new_temp(result)
+        self.push(SBin(dst, op, OC_INT, lhs, rhs))
+        dst
+    }
+}
+
+/// Whether `op` compares, giving a `Bool`.
+pub fn is_comparison(op: HirBinOp) -> Bool {
+    match op {
+        OpEq => true
+        OpNe => true
+        OpLt => true
+        OpLe => true
+        OpGt => true
+        OpGe => true
+        _ => false
+    }
+}
+
+// An optional temp as Rust's `Debug` prints one, for a panic's message.
+fn dest_debug(t: Int) -> String { if t < 0 { "None" } else { "Some(Temp(${t}))" } }
diff --git a/selfhost/src/lower_match.nova b/selfhost/src/lower_match.nova
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..766646b7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/selfhost/src/lower_match.nova
@@ -0,0 +1,382 @@
+// `match`: lower.rs's `lower_match`, `lower_switch_arms`,
+// `lower_string_match`, `lower_arm_body`, `lower_chain_arms`,
+// `lower_pattern_test`, `branch_or_fail`, `test_int_eq`, `test_int_cmp`
+// and `concrete`, function by function (spec
+// docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-14-phase-5-4-mir-design.md §6.1, ADR
+// 0057): a flat match on a sum, an `Int`, a `Char` or a `Bool` is one
+// `Switch`, on a `String` a chain of equality tests; a match with a guard
+// or a nested pattern lowers wholly by test chains.
+//
+// S0's `lower_switch_arms` takes the function that reads a pattern's
+// switch value; N passes which one, `KEY_VARIANT`, `KEY_INT` or
+// `KEY_BOOL` (`switch_key`).
+
+import hir
+import lower
+import lower_call
+import mir
+import token
+
+/// A variant pattern's switch value: its variant's index.
+pub const KEY_VARIANT: Int = 0
+
+/// An `Int` or `Char` literal pattern's switch value: its value.
+pub const KEY_INT: Int = 1
+
+/// A `Bool` literal pattern's switch value: 1 or 0.
+pub const KEY_BOOL: Int = 2
+
+/// The switch value of `pat` read as `kind` says, `None` for a catch-all
+/// (the closure S0 passes `lower_switch_arms`).
+pub fn switch_key(kind: Int, pat: HirPattern) -> Option<Int> {
+    if kind == KEY_VARIANT {
+        match pat {
+            PatVariant(_, variant, _) => Some(variant)
+            _ => None
+        }
+    } else if kind == KEY_INT {
+        match pat {
+            PatInt(v) => Some(v)
+            PatChar(c) => Some(c.to_int())
+            _ => None
+        }
+    } else {
+        match pat {
+            PatBool(v) => Some(if v { 1 } else { 0 })
+            _ => None
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Lowerer {
+    /// A `match` (`lower_match`).
+    pub fn lower_match(mut self, e: HirExpr, scrutinee: HirExpr, arms: Vec<HirArm>) -> Int {
+        let result = self.new_temp(mir_ty(e.ty))
+        let s = self.lower_expr(scrutinee)
+        if diverges(scrutinee) { return result }
+        let join = self.new_block()
+        // A match with a guard or a pattern the flat lowering does not take
+        // lowers wholly by test chains.
+        let mut flat = true
+        for a in arms.iter() {
+            if a.guard.is_some() || !is_flat(a.pattern) {
+                flat = false
+                break
+            }
+        }
+        if !flat {
+            self.lower_chain_arms(s, scrutinee.ty, arms, result, join)
+            self.switch_to(join)
+            return result
+        }
+        match scrutinee.ty {
+            TySum(_, _) => {
+                let disc = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+                self.push(SSumTag(disc, s))
+                self.lower_switch_arms(s, disc, arms, result, join, KEY_VARIANT)
+            }
+            TyInt => self.lower_switch_arms(s, s, arms, result, join, KEY_INT)
+            TyChar => self.lower_switch_arms(s, s, arms, result, join, KEY_INT)
+            TyBool => self.lower_switch_arms(s, s, arms, result, join, KEY_BOOL)
+            TyString => self.lower_string_match(s, arms, result, join)
+            _ => {
+                // Only catch-all arms are possible for other types.
+                if arms.is_empty() {
+                    self.terminate(TermTrap)
+                } else {
+                    self.lower_arm_body(s, arms.at(0), result)
+                    self.terminate(TermGoto(join))
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        self.switch_to(join)
+        result
+    }
+
+    /// Arms dispatched by an integer discriminant, a sum's tag or an
+    /// `Int`, `Char` or `Bool` value (`lower_switch_arms`): `key` reads a
+    /// pattern's switch value ([`switch_key`]).
+    pub fn lower_switch_arms(
+        mut self,
+        scrut: Int,
+        disc: Int,
+        arms: Vec<HirArm>,
+        result: Int,
+        join: Int,
+        key: Int,
+    ) {
+        let mut switch_arms: Vec<(Int, Int)> = Vec::new()
+        let mut default = -1
+        let mut pending: Vec<(Int, HirArm)> = Vec::new()
+        for arm in arms.iter() {
+            match switch_key(key, arm.pattern) {
+                Some(value) => {
+                    let mut seen = false
+                    for (v, _) in switch_arms.iter() { if v == value { seen = true } }
+                    // A duplicate arm is unreachable.
+                    if seen { continue }
+                    let b = self.new_block()
+                    switch_arms.push((value, b))
+                    pending.push((b, arm))
+                }
+                None => {
+                    // A catch-all, a binding or a wildcard, is the default;
+                    // later arms are unreachable.
+                    if default < 0 {
+                        let b = self.new_block()
+                        default = b
+                        pending.push((b, arm))
+                    }
+                    break
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if default < 0 {
+            // Exhaustiveness was checked: this is unreachable.
+            let b = self.new_block()
+            let saved = self.current
+            self.switch_to(b)
+            self.terminate(TermTrap)
+            self.switch_to(saved)
+            default = b
+        }
+        self.terminate(TermSwitch(disc, switch_arms, default))
+        for (block, arm) in pending.iter() {
+            self.switch_to(block)
+            self.lower_arm_body(scrut, arm, result)
+            self.terminate(TermGoto(join))
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// A `String` match, a chain of equality tests
+    /// (`lower_string_match`).
+    pub fn lower_string_match(mut self, scrut: Int, arms: Vec<HirArm>, result: Int, join: Int) {
+        for arm in arms.iter() {
+            match arm.pattern {
+                PatStr(lit) => {
+                    let lit_t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+                    self.push(SConstStr(lit_t, lit))
+                    let eq = self.new_temp(MIR_I8)
+                    self.push(SCallRuntime(eq, RT_STR_EQ, Vec::from_array([scrut, lit_t])))
+                    let body_b = self.new_block()
+                    let next = self.new_block()
+                    self.terminate(TermBranch(eq, body_b, next))
+                    self.switch_to(body_b)
+                    self.lower_arm_body(scrut, arm, result)
+                    self.terminate(TermGoto(join))
+                    self.switch_to(next)
+                }
+                _ => {
+                    // A catch-all ends the chain.
+                    self.lower_arm_body(scrut, arm, result)
+                    self.terminate(TermGoto(join))
+                    return
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // No catch-all: unreachable, exhaustiveness was checked.
+        self.terminate(TermTrap)
+    }
+
+    /// The arm's bindings, then its body, into the current block
+    /// (`lower_arm_body`).
+    pub fn lower_arm_body(mut self, scrut: Int, arm: HirArm, result: Int) {
+        match arm.pattern {
+            PatBind(local) => self.copy(self.local_map.at(local), scrut)
+            PatVariant(_, _, fields) => {
+                for i in 0..fields.len() {
+                    match fields.at(i) {
+                        PatBind(local) => {
+                            let dst = self.local_map.at(local)
+                            let ty = self.temps.at(dst)
+                            if ty != MIR_UNIT {
+                                let v = self.new_temp(ty)
+                                self.push(SSumField(v, scrut, i, ty))
+                                self.push(SCopy(dst, v))
+                            }
+                        }
+                        _ => ()
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            _ => ()
+        }
+        let v = self.lower_expr(arm.body)
+        if !diverges(arm.body) { self.copy(result, v) }
+    }
+
+    /// A `match` by test chains (`lower_chain_arms`): each arm in order
+    /// tests its pattern and then its guard, a failure jumping to the next
+    /// arm's chain; a trap after the last.
+    pub fn lower_chain_arms(
+        mut self,
+        scrut: Int,
+        ty: Ty,
+        arms: Vec<HirArm>,
+        result: Int,
+        join: Int,
+    ) {
+        for arm in arms.iter() {
+            let next = self.new_block()
+            self.lower_pattern_test(arm.pattern, scrut, ty, next)
+            match arm.guard {
+                Some(guard) => {
+                    let g = self.lower_expr(guard)
+                    if !diverges(guard) {
+                        let body = self.new_block()
+                        self.terminate(TermBranch(g, body, next))
+                        self.switch_to(body)
+                    }
+                }
+                None => ()
+            }
+            let v = self.lower_expr(arm.body)
+            if !diverges(arm.body) { self.copy(result, v) }
+            self.terminate(TermGoto(join))
+            self.switch_to(next)
+        }
+        // Exhaustiveness was checked: no value gets here.
+        self.terminate(TermTrap)
+    }
+
+    /// Test `value`, of type `ty`, against `pat` in the current block,
+    /// jumping to `fail` when it does not match (`lower_pattern_test`).
+    pub fn lower_pattern_test(mut self, pat: HirPattern, value: Int, ty: Ty, fail: Int) {
+        match pat {
+            PatWildcard => ()
+            PatBind(local) => self.copy(self.local_map.at(local), value)
+            PatInt(n) => self.test_int_eq(value, n, fail)
+            PatChar(c) => self.test_int_eq(value, c.to_int(), fail)
+            PatBool(b) => {
+                let ok = self.new_block()
+                if b {
+                    self.terminate(TermBranch(value, ok, fail))
+                } else {
+                    self.terminate(TermBranch(value, fail, ok))
+                }
+                self.switch_to(ok)
+            }
+            PatStr(lit) => {
+                let lit_t = self.new_temp(MIR_PTR)
+                self.push(SConstStr(lit_t, lit))
+                let eq = self.new_temp(MIR_I8)
+                self.push(SCallRuntime(eq, RT_STR_EQ, Vec::from_array([value, lit_t])))
+                self.branch_or_fail(eq, fail)
+            }
+            PatRange(lo, hi) => {
+                self.test_int_cmp(value, OpGe, lo, fail)
+                self.test_int_cmp(value, OpLe, hi, fail)
+            }
+            PatVariant(sum, variant, fields) => {
+                // A sum of one variant needs no tag test.
+                let def = self.program.sum(sum)
+                let tested = match def {
+                    Some(d) => d.variants.len() > 1
+                    None => true
+                }
+                if tested {
+                    let tag = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+                    self.push(SSumTag(tag, value))
+                    self.test_int_eq(tag, variant, fail)
+                }
+                let args = match ty {
+                    TySum(_, a) => a
+                    _ => Vec::new()
+                }
+                let mut field_tys: Vec<Ty> = Vec::new()
+                match def {
+                    Some(d) => {
+                        if variant >= 0 && variant < d.variants.len() {
+                            for f in d.variants.at(variant).fields.iter() {
+                                field_tys.push(self.concrete(f, args))
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                    None => ()
+                }
+                for i in 0..fields.len() {
+                    let field = fields.at(i)
+                    match field {
+                        PatWildcard => continue
+                        _ => ()
+                    }
+                    let fty = if i < field_tys.len() { field_tys.at(i) } else { TyError }
+                    let mty = mir_ty(fty)
+                    let t = if mty == MIR_UNIT {
+                        self.unit_temp()
+                    } else {
+                        let t = self.new_temp(mty)
+                        self.push(SSumField(t, value, i, mty))
+                        t
+                    }
+                    self.lower_pattern_test(field, t, fty, fail)
+                }
+            }
+            PatTuple(elems) => {
+                let tys = match ty {
+                    TyTuple(ts) => ts
+                    _ => Vec::new()
+                }
+                for i in 0..elems.len() {
+                    let elem = elems.at(i)
+                    match elem {
+                        PatWildcard => continue
+                        _ => ()
+                    }
+                    let ety = if i < tys.len() { tys.at(i) } else { TyError }
+                    let mty = mir_ty(ety)
+                    let t = if mty == MIR_UNIT {
+                        self.unit_temp()
+                    } else {
+                        let t = self.new_temp(mty)
+                        self.push(SRecordField(t, value, i, mty))
+                        t
+                    }
+                    self.lower_pattern_test(elem, t, ety, fail)
+                }
+            }
+            PatOr(alts) => {
+                let ok = self.new_block()
+                for k in 0..alts.len() {
+                    let last = k + 1 == alts.len()
+                    let alt_fail = if last { fail } else { self.new_block() }
+                    self.lower_pattern_test(alts.at(k), value, ty, alt_fail)
+                    self.terminate(TermGoto(ok))
+                    if !last { self.switch_to(alt_fail) }
+                }
+                self.switch_to(ok)
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Go on in a fresh block when `cond` holds; jump to `fail` when not
+    /// (`branch_or_fail`).
+    pub fn branch_or_fail(mut self, cond: Int, fail: Int) {
+        let ok = self.new_block()
+        self.terminate(TermBranch(cond, ok, fail))
+        self.switch_to(ok)
+    }
+
+    /// `value == n`, an `Int`, `Char` or tag test (`test_int_eq`).
+    pub fn test_int_eq(mut self, value: Int, n: Int, fail: Int) {
+        self.test_int_cmp(value, OpEq, n, fail)
+    }
+
+    /// `value op n` on integers, failing to `fail` (`test_int_cmp`).
+    pub fn test_int_cmp(mut self, value: Int, op: HirBinOp, n: Int, fail: Int) {
+        let c = self.new_temp(MIR_I64)
+        self.push(SConstInt(c, n))
+        let cond = self.bin_i64(op, value, c, MIR_I8)
+        self.branch_or_fail(cond, fail)
+    }
+
+    /// A field's declared type at the scrutinee's type arguments, any
+    /// projection normalized (`concrete`).
+    pub fn concrete(self, field: Ty, args: Vec<Ty>) -> Ty {
+        let ty = subst(field, args)
+        match normalize_ty(ty, self.program.impls) {
+            Ok(t) => t
+            Err(_) => ty
+        }
+    }
+}

--- a/selfhost/src/main.nova

@@ -33,6 +33,18 @@

 //                             program, its body checked, std's bodies

+//                             prints the MIR dump of each program whose
+//                             root is FILE, after the same header, as
+//                             `nova check --dump-mir --before-async`
+//                             does: natively, monomorphized from `main`

+//                             std's checked functions spliced into each
+//                             program (spec
+//                             docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-14-phase-5-4-mir-design.md
+//                             §5, §6, ADR 0057); without `--before-async`
+//                             it refuses with the exit code 3 until N's

 //
 // A file with lex or parse errors is dumped with them, and the exit code
 // stays 0. A file that cannot be read prints `IOERR` and its kind after its
@@ -51,12 +63,19 @@ import lexer
 import lexer_tests
 import loader

+import lower
+import lower_call

 import manifest
+import mir

+import mono

+import splice

@@ -83,6 +102,10 @@ async fn main() {
         dump_command(argv, command).await
         return
     }

+        mir_command(argv).await

     // No file is refused, as S0's clap refuses it.
     let tokens = command == "tokens"

@@ -248,6 +271,199 @@ async fn dump_command(argv: Vec<String>, command: String) {
