  Any difference is a change spec §3 does not allow: stop and record it.
- [ ] **Step 5: The hidden flags stay hidden.**
  - `helpcmp.sh`: `--help` of `nova` and of every command, both sides:

```sh
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$P/plan54/helpcmp.sh")"
cat > "$P/plan54/helpcmp.sh" <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
# helpcmp.sh: run from the repository's root, after the build, with $P exported.
# `--help` of `nova` and of every command, by the base's `nova`
# ($P/nova-base-54/nova, Task 0) and the tree's `target/debug/nova`, both run
# through a link named `nova` so the usage lines agree: both streams and the
# exit status must be byte-identical, the hidden flags and `more` absent
# from `parse --help` and `check --help`.
set -u
: "${P:?set P to the scratch directory}"
[ -f Cargo.toml ] && [ -x target/debug/nova ] || { echo "run from the repository's root, after the build"; exit 1; }
d="$P/help-54"
rm -rf "$d"; mkdir -p "$d/base" "$d/tree"
ln -s "$P/nova-base-54/nova" "$d/base/nova"
ln -s "$PWD/target/debug/nova" "$d/tree/nova"
n=0; same=0
for cmd in "" parse run build bundle dev check test fmt doc lsp new init add remove fetch update login package publish version help; do
  a=$("$d/base/nova" $cmd --help 2>&1; echo "exit=$?")
  b=$("$d/tree/nova" $cmd --help 2>&1; echo "exit=$?")
  n=$((n + 1))
  if [ "$a" == "$b" ]; then same=$((same + 1)); else echo "differs: nova $cmd --help"; fi
done
hidden=$( ("$d/tree/nova" parse --help; "$d/tree/nova" check --help) | grep -c 'dump-\|MORE\|--std\|--test\|before-async')
echo "help: $same of $n identical; hidden flags shown: $hidden"
rm -rf "$d"
[ "$same" = "$n" ] && [ "$hidden" = 0 ]
EOF
```

  - `usagecmp.sh`: every refusal `parse` and `check` give, both sides,
    through `argv0.py`, which runs a program by execv with a first
    argument of its choosing:

```sh
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$P/plan54/argv0.py")"
cat > "$P/plan54/argv0.py" <<'EOF'
"""argv0.py ARGV0 PROGRAM ARG...: run PROGRAM, by execv, with ARGV0 as its
first argument and the ARGs after it. ARGV0 is written with Python's
escapes, so '\xff\xfe' is those two bytes, and may be empty, which
os.execv refuses: libc's execv is called through ctypes."""
import ctypes
import sys

arg0 = sys.argv[1].encode().decode("unicode_escape").encode("latin-1")
argv = [arg0] + [a.encode() for a in sys.argv[3:]]
array = (ctypes.c_char_p * (len(argv) + 1))(*argv, None)
libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
libc.execv(sys.argv[2].encode(), array)
sys.exit(f"execv failed: errno {ctypes.get_errno()}")
EOF
```

```sh
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$P/plan54/usagecmp.sh")"
cat > "$P/plan54/usagecmp.sh" <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
# usagecmp.sh (5.4: `--dump-mir`'s shapes beside `--dump-hir`'s and `--dump-resolution`'s; from 5.3b's): run from the repository's root, after the build, with $P exported
# and $P/plan54/argv0.py written. The errors `parse` and `check` give for
# arguments they refuse, and what they print for the shapes beside them,
# from the base's `nova` ($P/nova-base-54/nova, Task 0) and the tree's
# `target/debug/nova`, each run by execv with the same first argument:
# `nova` (a link's name), `othernova` (a renamed binary), and four with no
# file name in UTF-8, "", "/", ".." and the bytes ff fe. Both streams and
# the exit status must be byte-identical, tips, usage lines and `--help`
# included: every `check` command line whose first argument after `check`
# is none of `--dump-resolution`, `--dump-hir` and `--dump-mir` is parsed
# as the base parses it. Then the
# same in a terminal, colours included, through `script`'s pseudo-terminal
# with NO_COLOR unset. Then 5.1's `--dump-ast` against the tree's own
# `--dump-tokens`. Then the shapes the hidden `check --dump-resolution`
# and `check --dump-hir` refuse, and since 5.4 `check --dump-mir`'s, which
# the base lacks: each must exit 2 with nothing on stdout and one error on
# stderr, no panic, and a refusal of `--dump-hir` or `--dump-mir` names its
# flag (in its usage line, or first in its own error) and never another
# hidden flag but as the argument refused.
set -u
: "${P:?set P to the scratch directory}"
[ -f Cargo.toml ] && [ -x target/debug/nova ] || { echo "run from the repository's root, after the build"; exit 1; }
nova="$PWD/target/debug/nova"
work=$(mktemp -d) && cd "$work" || exit 1
shapes=("parse a b" "parse --ast a b" "parse --check a b" "parse -- a b"
  "check a b" "check a ''" "check --std" "check --std a" "check -- a b"
  "check --target wasm a b" "check a --std"
  "check --st a.nova" "check --stdd a.nova" "check --std-part a.nova" "check --dump a.nova"
  "check --dump-res a.nova" "check --dump-resolutio a.nova" "check --std=1 a.nova"
  "check --dump-resolution=1 a.nova" "check --std --std a.nova" "check a.nova b.nova --target bogus"
  "check --std --help" "check --std -h" "check a b --bogus" "check a.nova --dump-resolution"
  "check --target native --dump-resolution a.nova" "check -- --dump-resolution"
  "check --help --dump-resolution" "check -h --std"
  "check --test a" "check --test" "check a --test" "check --test --dump-resolution a" "check --tests a"
  "check --test=1 a" "check -- --test" "check --test ''" "check --test -- a" "check '' --test"
  "check --test --test a" "check a --test --dump-resolution"
  "check --dump-hi a.nova" "check --dump-hirr a.nova" "check --dump-hir=1 a.nova" "check --dump-hir= a.nova"
  "check a.nova --dump-hir" "check --target native --dump-hir a.nova" "check -- --dump-hir"
  "check --help --dump-hir" "check --test --dump-hir a" "check a --test --dump-hir"
  "check --dump-hir-item a.nova" "check --dump-hir-itemss a.nova" "check --dump-hir-items=1 a.nova"
  "check a.nova --dump-hir-items" "check -- --dump-hir-items" "check --help --dump-hir-items"
  "check --dump-hir-items a.nova" "check --dump-hir-items" "check --dump-hir-items --std" "check --dump-hir-items --test a"
  "check --dump-hir-items --target wasm a" "check --dump-hir-items --help" "check --dump-hir-items -h"
  "check --dump-mi a.nova" "check --dump-mirr a.nova" "check --dump-mir=1 a.nova" "check --dump-mir= a.nova"
  "check --dump-mir=before-async a.nova" "check a.nova --dump-mir" "check --target native --dump-mir a.nova"
  "check -- --dump-mir" "check --help --dump-mir" "check --test --dump-mir a" "check a --test --dump-mir"
  "check --before-async a.nova" "check --before-async" "check a --before-async" "check --dump-mir-before a.nova")
runs=0; same=0
for arg0 in nova othernova "" / .. '\xff\xfe'; do
  for args in "${shapes[@]}"; do
    eval "python3 -I '$P/plan54/argv0.py' '$arg0' '$P/nova-base-54/nova' $args" > "$P/usage-base.txt" 2>&1
    echo "exit $?" >> "$P/usage-base.txt"
    eval "python3 -I '$P/plan54/argv0.py' '$arg0' '$nova' $args" > "$P/usage-tree.txt" 2>&1
    echo "exit $?" >> "$P/usage-tree.txt"
    runs=$((runs + 1))
    if cmp -s "$P/usage-base.txt" "$P/usage-tree.txt"; then same=$((same + 1)); else
      echo "differs: [$arg0] $args"; diff "$P/usage-base.txt" "$P/usage-tree.txt" | head -12; fi
  done
  echo "[$arg0] $(grep -a '^Usage:' "$P/usage-tree.txt" | head -1), $(tail -1 "$P/usage-tree.txt")"
done
tty=0; ttys=("parse a b" "parse -- a b" "check a b" "check --std a" "check --st a.nova"
  "check --std=1 a.nova" "check --std --help" "check a b --bogus" "check --test a" "check a --test")
for args in "${ttys[@]}"; do
  env -u NO_COLOR script -qc "python3 -I '$P/plan54/argv0.py' othernova '$P/nova-base-54/nova' $args" /dev/null > "$P/usage-base.txt"
  env -u NO_COLOR script -qc "python3 -I '$P/plan54/argv0.py' othernova '$nova' $args" /dev/null > "$P/usage-tree.txt"
  if cmp -s "$P/usage-base.txt" "$P/usage-tree.txt"; then tty=$((tty + 1)); else echo "differs in a terminal: $args"; fi
done
grep -qa $'\e\\[' "$P/usage-tree.txt" || echo "no colour in the terminal's output"
asts=0; astsame=0
for arg0 in othernova "" / .. '\xff\xfe'; do
  for args in "parse --dump-ast --ast a b" "parse --dump-ast --check a" "parse --dump-ast" "parse --dump-ast ''" "parse --dump-ast=1 a"; do
    tok=${args//dump-ast/dump-tokens}
    eval "python3 -I '$P/plan54/argv0.py' '$arg0' '$nova' $args" > "$P/usage-base.txt" 2>&1
    echo "exit $?" >> "$P/usage-base.txt"
    eval "python3 -I '$P/plan54/argv0.py' '$arg0' '$nova' $tok" 2>&1 | sed 's/dump-tokens/dump-ast/g' > "$P/usage-tree.txt"
    echo "exit ${PIPESTATUS[0]}" >> "$P/usage-tree.txt"
    asts=$((asts + 1))
    if cmp -s "$P/usage-base.txt" "$P/usage-tree.txt"; then astsame=$((astsame + 1)); else
      echo "--dump-ast differs: [$arg0] $args"; diff "$P/usage-base.txt" "$P/usage-tree.txt"; fi
  done
done
res=0; resok=0; hir=0; hirok=0
for arg0 in nova othernova "" / .. '\xff\xfe'; do
  for flag in --dump-hir --dump-mir; do
  for rest in "" "--std a" "--dump-hir a" "--dump-resolution a" "--target bogus a" "--std --std" "--stdd" "--help" "-h" \
              "--test --test a" "--test=1 a" "--tests a" "--test --target wasm a" "--target=wasm --test --std" \
              "--test --std a" "--test" "--test -h" "--test --std --target native a" "--target wasm --test" \
              "--test --std --test" "--target= a" "--before-async --before-async a" "--before-async=1 a" \
              "--before-asyn a" "--before-async" "--dump-mir a" "--target native a" "--test a" "--std"; do
    # The HIR dump takes these (5.4's MIR-only refusals).
    case "$flag:$rest" in "--dump-hir:--target native a"|"--dump-hir:--test a"|"--dump-hir:--std") continue;; esac
    eval "python3 -I '$P/plan54/argv0.py' '$arg0' '$nova' check $flag $rest" > "$P/usage-tree.txt" 2> "$P/usage-err.txt"
    code=$?
    hir=$((hir + 1))
    named=$( (grep -q '^Usage: ' "$P/usage-err.txt" && grep -q "^Usage: nova check $flag " "$P/usage-err.txt") \
      || grep -q "^error: $flag " "$P/usage-err.txt" \
      || (grep -q "possible values" "$P/usage-err.txt" && ! grep -q '^Usage: ' "$P/usage-err.txt"); echo $?)
    stray=0
    for other in --dump-resolution --dump-hir --dump-mir; do
      [ "$other" = "$flag" ] && continue
      stray=$((stray + $(grep -v "'$other'" "$P/usage-err.txt" | grep -c -- "$other ")))
    done
    if [ "$code" = 2 ] && [ ! -s "$P/usage-tree.txt" ] && [ "$(grep -c '^error' "$P/usage-err.txt")" = 1 ] \
       && ! grep -q panicked "$P/usage-err.txt" && [ "$named" = 0 ] && [ "$stray" = 0 ]; then hirok=$((hirok + 1)); else
      echo "$flag: [$arg0] $rest: exit $code"; cat "$P/usage-tree.txt" "$P/usage-err.txt"; fi
  done
  done
  for args in "check --dump-resolution" "check --dump-resolution --std a" "check --dump-resolution --dump-resolution a" \
              "check --dump-resolution --target bogus a" "check --dump-resolution --std --std" \
              "check --dump-resolution --stdd" "check --dump-resolution --help" "check --dump-resolution -h" \
              "check --dump-resolution --test --test a" "check --dump-resolution --test=1 a" \
              "check --dump-resolution --tests a" "check --dump-resolution --test --target wasm a" \
              "check --dump-resolution --target=wasm --test --std" "check --dump-resolution --test --std a" \
              "check --dump-resolution --test" "check --dump-resolution --test -h" \
              "check --dump-resolution --test --std --target native a" "check --dump-resolution --target wasm --test" \
              "check --dump-resolution --test --std --test"; do
    eval "python3 -I '$P/plan54/argv0.py' '$arg0' '$nova' $args" > "$P/usage-tree.txt" 2> "$P/usage-err.txt"
    code=$?
    res=$((res + 1))
    if [ "$code" = 2 ] && [ ! -s "$P/usage-tree.txt" ] && [ "$(grep -c '^error' "$P/usage-err.txt")" = 1 ] \
       && ! grep -q panicked "$P/usage-err.txt"; then resok=$((resok + 1)); else
      echo "--dump-resolution: [$arg0] $args: exit $code"; cat "$P/usage-tree.txt" "$P/usage-err.txt"; fi
  done
done
echo "usage: $same of $runs runs identical; in a terminal, $tty of ${#ttys[@]}; --dump-ast as --dump-tokens: $astsame of $asts; --dump-resolution refused: $resok of $res; --dump-hir and --dump-mir refused: $hirok of $hir"
rm -f "$P/usage-base.txt" "$P/usage-tree.txt" "$P/usage-err.txt"; cd / && rmdir "$work"
[ "$same" = "$runs" ] && [ "$tty" = "${#ttys[@]}" ] && [ "$astsame" = "$asts" ] && [ "$resok" = "$res" ] && [ "$hirok" = "$hir" ]
EOF
```

```sh
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$P/plan54/lists.py")"
cat > "$P/plan54/lists.py" <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""lists.py: run from the repository's root. The loose corpus as the
harness lists it (`loose_corpus`: a sorted walk, `selfhost/` and the cases
left out, and no file of a package's `src/` or `tests/`), one per line."""
import os

ROOT = os.getcwd()
CASES = os.path.join(ROOT, "crates/nova-cli/tests/selfhost/cases")


def nova_files(d, skip, out):
    for name in sorted(os.listdir(d)):
        p = os.path.join(d, name)
        if os.path.islink(p):
            continue
        if os.path.isdir(p):
            if name in ("target", "node_modules") or name.startswith(".") or p in skip:
                continue
            nova_files(p, skip, out)
        elif os.path.isfile(p) and name.endswith(".nova"):
            out.append(os.path.relpath(p, ROOT))


def in_package(f):
    parent = os.path.dirname(os.path.join(ROOT, f))
    return (os.path.basename(parent) in ("src", "tests")
            and os.path.isfile(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(parent), "nova.toml")))


files = []
nova_files(ROOT, [os.path.join(ROOT, "selfhost"), CASES], files)
for f in files:
    if not in_package(f):
        print(f)
EOF
```

```sh
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$P/plan54/mem.py")"
cat > "$P/plan54/mem.py" <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""mem.py COLUMN BINARY nova|n|nr: run from the repository's root, after
`SH` has written the MIR fuzzer's and the depth test's inputs, with $P
exported and $P/plan54/loose.txt written (lists.py). The peak RSS
(/proc/PID/status's VmHWM, polled every 2 ms: wait4's ru_maxrss counts the
pages a child holds from its Python parent before exec) of BINARY's MIR
dump, S0's `check --dump-mir` (`nova`) or N's `mir --std std` (`n`, `nr`), on
ADR 0057 §18's inputs: N's root after and before the transform, the loose
corpus, the fuzzer's after form and the depth cases; with `n`, also
NOVA_GC_DEBUG's collections and largest live heap (the debug runtime's).
Appends one line a set to $P/plan54/mem.txt, headed by COLUMN."""
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

P = os.environ["P"]
ROOT = os.getcwd()
col, binary, kind = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
TMP = tempfile.gettempdir()
FZ, DP = os.path.join(TMP, "nova-selfhost-mir-fuzz"), os.path.join(TMP, "nova-selfhost-deep-n-mir")
fz = [f"{s}/{f}" for s in (601, 602, 603) for f in sorted(os.listdir(f"{FZ}/{s}"))]
dp = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(DP) if f.endswith(".nova"))
loose = open(f"{P}/plan54/loose.txt").read().split()
SETS = [("N's root, after form", ROOT, ["selfhost/src/main.nova"], []),
        ("N's root, before form", ROOT, ["selfhost/src/main.nova"], ["--before-async"]),
        ("the loose corpus", ROOT, loose, []),
        ("the fuzzer, after form", FZ, fz, []),
        ("the depth cases", DP, dp, [])]


def argv(flags, files):
    if kind == "nova":
        return [binary, "check", "--dump-mir", *flags, *files]
    return [binary, "mir", "--std", f"{ROOT}/std", *flags, *files]


def hwm(cmd, cwd):
    pr = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=cwd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    peak = 0
    while pr.poll() is None:
        try:
            for line in open(f"/proc/{pr.pid}/status"):
                if line.startswith("VmHWM:"):
                    peak = max(peak, int(line.split()[1]))
        except OSError:
            pass
        time.sleep(0.002)
    return peak / 1024, pr.returncode


with open(f"{P}/plan54/mem.txt", "a") as out:
    for name, cwd, files, flags in SETS:
        mb, code = hwm(argv(flags, files), cwd)
        gc = ""
        if kind == "n":  # the debug runtime; `nr`, the release one, is RSS alone
            r = subprocess.run(argv(flags, files), cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True,
                               env=dict(os.environ, NOVA_GC_DEBUG="1"))
            lives = [int(m.group(1)) for m in re.finditer(r"nova-gc: collection[^(]*\((\d+)", r.stderr)]
            gc = f" | {len(lives)} collections, largest live {max(lives, default=0) / 1e6:.1f} MB (10^6 bytes)"
        line = f"{col} | {name} ({len(files)}) | {mb:.0f} MB exit={code}{gc}"
        print(line, flush=True)
        out.write(line + "\n")
EOF
```

```sh
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$P/plan54/stack.py")"
cat > "$P/plan54/stack.py" <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""stack.py NBIN OUT [FILE...]: run from the repository's root, after the
build and after `SH` wrote the depth test's inputs (part 4's script). The
least main-thread stack (KB, 4 KB bisection under `ulimit -s`) on which N's
`selfhost mir --std std FILE` exits 0 and prints S0's part, for each depth
input in the temporary directory's `nova-selfhost-deep-n-mir` (every one
when no FILE is named), appended to OUT a line each: the file and the KB,
or `FAILS at 1024`."""
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile

ROOT = os.getcwd()
D = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "nova-selfhost-deep-n-mir")
STD = os.path.join(ROOT, "std")
NB, OUT = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
files = sys.argv[3:] or sorted(f for f in os.listdir(D) if f.endswith(".nova"))


def s0(f):
    return subprocess.run([os.path.join(ROOT, "target/debug/nova"), "check", "--dump-mir", f], cwd=D,
                          capture_output=True).stdout


def ok(f, kb, want):
    r = subprocess.run(["sh", "-c", 'ulimit -s %d && exec "$0" "$@"' % kb, NB, "mir", "--std", STD, f],
                       cwd=D, capture_output=True)
    return r.returncode == 0 and r.stdout == want


with open(OUT, "a") as out:
    for f in files:
        want = s0(f)
        lo, hi = 4, 1024  # lo fails (or untested), hi passes
        if not ok(f, hi, want):
            out.write("%s\tFAILS at 1024\n" % f)
            out.flush()
            continue
        while hi - lo > 4:
            mid = (lo + hi) // 2 // 4 * 4
            if mid <= lo:
                mid = lo + 4
            if ok(f, mid, want):
                hi = mid
            else:
                lo = mid
        out.write("%s\t%d\n" % (f, hi))
        out.flush()
EOF
```

    ```sh
    mkdir -p "$P/plan54/heap"
    (cd selfhost && NOVA_RUNTIME_LIB="$PWD/../target/debug/libnova_runtime.a" ../target/debug/nova build -o "$P/plan54/heap/n")
    python3 "$P/plan54/lists.py" > "$P/plan54/loose.txt"
    : > "$P/plan54/mem.txt"
    python3 "$P/plan54/mem.py" "N debug" "$P/plan54/heap/n" n
    python3 "$P/plan54/mem.py" "S0 opt 0" "$PWD/target/debug/nova" nova
    : > "$P/plan54/stack-debug.tsv"
    python3 "$P/plan54/stack.py" "$P/plan54/heap/n" "$P/plan54/stack-debug.tsv"
    ```

    Expected, as measured at `430618f5`, whose N, build and inputs are
    the last commit's (`stack.py` 165 s there): `loose.txt` 251 lines;
    `mem.py`'s lines, N on the debug runtime: N's root after the
    transform 208 MB, `23 collections, largest live 105.4 MB (10^6
    bytes)`; before it 172 MB, 23 and 104.0; the loose corpus 41 MB,
    103 and 11.3; the fuzzer's after form 41 MB, 62 and 12.3; the depth
    cases 41 MB, 42 and 11.7; S0 at `opt-level = 0` 118, 117, 45, 44 and
    47 MB; `stack-debug.tsv`'s 25 lines, none `FAILS at 1024`, 48 to 212
    KB.
  - Then as the Selfhost job runs, in one shell: `export
    NOVA_EMBED_RUNTIME=1 CARGO_PROFILE_DEV_OPT_LEVEL=1`, `df -h /` (at
    least 6G), `time cargo build --locked --workspace` (62 s at
    `430618f5`), `time cargo test --locked --workspace --test selfhost
    --no-run` (48 s there),
    then three times `time SH`; then N's tests on each runtime:
    `(cd selfhost && NOVA_RUNTIME_LIB=$PWD/../target/debug/libnova_runtime.a
    ../target/debug/nova test) 2>&1 | tail -n 1` and `(cd selfhost &&
    ../target/debug/nova test) 2>&1 | tail -n 1`; then the job's last
    step, `time NOVA_RUNTIME_LIB=$PWD/target/debug/libnova_runtime.a cargo
    test --locked --workspace --all-features --no-fail-fast --test selfhost
    -- --exact the_selfhost_tests_pass`. Expected: `64 passed; 0 failed`
    three times, about 62 s each (62.75, 61.08 and 62.08 s, user CPU 211
    to 218 s, at `430618f5`, before the report's test, which takes no
    time to speak of); `102
    passed` on each runtime; `1 passed` in 1.97 s (at `430618f5`).
  - Then, in the same shell, N on the release runtime and S0 at
    `opt-level = 1`:

    ```sh
    (cd selfhost && ../target/debug/nova build -o "$P/plan54/heap/n-rel")
    cp target/debug/nova "$P/plan54/heap/nova-ci"
    python3 "$P/plan54/mem.py" "N release" "$P/plan54/heap/n-rel" nr
    python3 "$P/plan54/mem.py" "S0 opt 1" "$P/plan54/heap/nova-ci" nova
    : > "$P/plan54/stack-release.tsv"
    python3 "$P/plan54/stack.py" "$P/plan54/heap/n-rel" "$P/plan54/stack-release.tsv"
    ```

    Expected, as measured at `430618f5`: N on the release runtime 206,
    173, 40, 38 and 39 MB; S0 at `opt-level = 1` 110, 109, 37, 36 and 36
    MB; `stack-release.tsv`'s 25 lines (each file emptied first, as
    `mem.txt` is: `stack.py` appends), none failing, 48 to 212 KB (43 s).
    Against ADR 0057 §17 and §18, as they record it measured again at
    `430618f5`: the same collections; the peaks within
    2 MB but N's root's before form (172 and 173 MB against 177 and 176);
    S0's within 3 MB; the largest live heaps the table's but N's root's
    before form, 104 against 120 MB, another moment of its last
    collection; each least stack within two bisection steps (8 KB) of
    §17's table. A change past 16 KB, an overflow at 1 MB, or a live heap
    that moves is recorded in the ledger; it is no threshold (spec §8).
  - Then `unset NOVA_EMBED_RUNTIME CARGO_PROFILE_DEV_OPT_LEVEL` and
    `cargo build --locked --workspace`, which ends with `Finished`: the
    debug-runtime `nova` at `opt-level = 0` again (`target/debug/nova
    version` prints `runtime: not embedded`).
- [ ] **Step 8: The mutants.** One at a time, each edit is made, its
  commands run, and the file restored with `git checkout -- <file>`, all
  on the last commit, in a shell under the process limit, with
  `NOVA_EMBED_RUNTIME=1` exported for the whole step, as the Selfhost job
  runs `selfhost.rs` (decision 21), and these two lists set:

  ```sh
  MIR="the_mir_dump_flag_is_hidden_and_refused_as_the_spec_says the_mir_dump_has_a_header_per_root_and_says_how_far_each_got every_run_of_a_mir_dump_settles_e0004s_paths_on_windows both_sides_refuse_the_mir_dumps_shapes_alike the_mir_dumps_agree_on_the_corpus_and_the_cases the_mir_dumps_agree_on_the_cases_with_windows_line_endings the_mir_fuzzers_inputs_agree_and_hold_each_kind n_lowers_the_depth_cases_on_a_1_mb_main_thread a_manifest_n_cannot_read_stops_n_with_a_message n_checks_std_for_each_program_when_std_gives_a_body_diagnostic the_selfhost_tests_pass"
  GUARD=the_resolution_dump_has_a_header_per_root_and_std_its_own_part
  ```

  - Each mutant runs only the tests it should trip and `$GUARD`, a cheap
    test that builds N and compares its resolution dump with S0's, which
    must pass every time: a mutant that broke everything would fail it.
    The whole of `SH` and `S` would take hours over 189 mutants; the
    replay's runs add up to 147 minutes this way.
  - Run them in batches sized by the replay's times, so that each
    batch, with half again, fits under 600 s: by the worst run of that
    many consecutive mutants in the replay, each batch one command in
    the foreground (Conventions):
    - at most six of S0's printer, 1 to 21 (the worst six in a row took
      338 s; 52 s each on average);
    - at most eight of S0's hook, flag and `MirDumps`, 22 to 39 but 34
      (374 s; 45 s each), and mutant 34 (138 s) with the guard's two, 58
      and 59 (165 s);
    - at most two of the splice, 40 to 57 (294 s; 137 s each, the
      driver's guard among their tests);
    - at most nine of N's, 60 to 155 (369 s; 36 s each);
    - the harness's 22 in two, 156 to 163 (347 s) and 164 to 177 (281
      s);
    - the MIR mismatch report's 12, 178 to 189, in one (142 s; 11.8 s
      each on average).
    If a batch is killed, or the shell restarts, `git checkout -- <file>`
    the file of the mutant it was on, see `git status --short` print
    nothing, and run that mutant again before the next batch.
  - Each edit replaces one exact text, which occurs once in its file
    unless the mutant names which occurrence, counted on the text as
    shown ("the second of 2"); "the line … goes" removes that whole
    line.
  - Each command list starts with `cargo build --locked --workspace`. A
    mutant of N needs no Rust rebuilt: `selfhost.rs` builds N from
    `selfhost/` each run.
  - Each caught mutant must fail as written: the tests named, and no
    other among those run; a name after `the_selfhost_tests_pass` in
    parentheses is one of N's `@test`s, which that test runs. Nine pass
    by design, each with its reason below: mutants 67, 75, 117, 156, 161,
    165, 166, 172 and 177. Any other mutant that passes is a gap: record
    it in the ledger, name it in the PR, and give it to the review (Step
    10), which says whether a check is added.
  - At the end, `git status --short` prints nothing, then `unset
    NOVA_EMBED_RUNTIME` and `cargo build --locked --workspace`.

- [ ] **Step 9: The review's inputs.** The full run's figures, the new
  tests by binary and `selfhost.rs`'s apart, the golden suite's line and
  time, the parse, check, package, help and usage comparisons, the
  differential's times, N's memory and stack, and the mutants' outcome,
  with each gap, go in the ledger.
- [ ] **Step 10: One fresh read-only review, then one fix pass** (the
  user's workflow).
  - The reviewer reads `git diff $BASE..HEAD` (Task 0) against the spec
    and its amendment, ADR 0057 and this plan, and is told to hunt for:
    - a Windows-only difference: a root list past `HIR_ARGS_LIMIT`, a
      path that reaches the MIR dump beyond the loader's E0004, a
      comparison not taken from `run`, a `\r\n` copy that changes nothing
      (Review Focus 1);
    - the splice: an id the shift leaves or moves wrongly on either side,
      a std function written in place, the order, a program's state
      reaching std's shared functions or the next program, the fallback's
      triggers in S0's `HirDumps` and N's `mir_of` and `mir_command`
      (Review Focus 2);
    - N's port against S0's, word for word: each MIR code's message,
      place and note, `mangle_ty`'s codes, the specializer's new nodes,
      the async transform's slots, tags, wrapper and order, the shim, the
      asserts (Review Focus 3);
    - the dump: a form missing from `KINDS`, a field printed from the
      wrong value or in the wrong order, a float, a string's quoting, the
      two printers apart (Review Focus 4);
    - the before form: the hook's place against the shim, the
      before-form set, the forms equal where no poll function is made
      (Review Focus 5);
    - argument handling: `--dump-mir` shown in a help or usage line, a
      shape refused differently from the spec, `--dump-mir=…` taken
      (Review Focus 6);
    - how far each root got: `runs=false` decided after the loader,
      `checked=false` missing an error, the entry's `main` (Review
      Focus 7);
    - a way for the harness to pass when the two sides differ: a set,
      case directory or form not compared, a count or floor dropped, a
      fuzzer input that does not lower, a depth case not walked (Review
      Focus 8);
    - the MIR mismatch report: a rule of decision 25 that the walk breaks,
      a place read past the nearest function or from the wrong side, a
      header or a line outside any function named wrongly, another dump's
      report changed (Review Focus 8);
    - the CI job: the comment against the tests it names; N's stack,
      memory and time against ADR 0057 §17 to §19 (Review Focus 9;
      decision 22);
    - a commit between Task 1 and Task 7 that builds wrong code or fails
      the suite, and the transitional mechanism the head does not have
      (decisions 16 and 17);
    - the records: ADR 0057, the CHANGELOG, the dated notes, the spec's
      amendment, every count (251 loose programs and 10 packages' mains,
      342 HIR cases and 86 MIR cases, 428 in all, 20 with a poll
      function, 86 roots taking the full check natively, 678 lowering,
      27 statements with 26 native, 120 fuzzer inputs, 25 depth
      programs) and figure against the tree, CI's runs (decisions 10 and
      11);
    - each mutant of Step 8 that passes, the accepted ones included, and
      whether a check is added;
    - MSRV 1.78, a new crate, a change to `Cargo.lock` or to a manifest,
      a test that touches the internet, a temporary name that is not
      fixed or missing from the Global Constraints' list.
  - Each finding is fixed in one commit, `5.4: the review's fixes`, or
    answered in the ledger with the reason it stands.
  - After the fixes, Steps 1 and 2 run again, and `SH`.

- [ ] **Step 12: The merge,** only on the user's word.
  - By rebase, pinned to the PR's head commit. Never auto-merge.
  - Then `git fetch origin main` and `git diff <head> origin/main --stat`,
